Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The grammars variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 859 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 418 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 859 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the grammars structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 418. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
